Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The bose-einstein variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 912 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 264 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on bose-einstein structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 912. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 264. QED.
